    VAR
      "--- dynamic commerce ---"@1000000000 : Integer;
      WebshopManagement@1000000001 : Codeunit 5447300;
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000002 : Codeunit 5447301;
      WebshopWebform@1000000003 : Page 5447366;
      WebformType@1000000004 : 'AttributeIcon,CouponBackground,CategoryImage,CategoryDesc,ItemDesc,ItemFile,TextModul,Placeholder,TxtModAttac,CampaignImages,Collection,ShippingOption,PaymentOption,ShippingOptionIcon,PaymentOptionIcon,Marketplace,SalesPerson';
      Text5447300@1000000005 : TextConst 'DEU=Verk�uferbild gel�scht;ENU=Salesperson image deleted';

    PROCEDURE EditContent@1000000000();
    BEGIN

      TESTFIELD(Code);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.SetSendNow(TRUE);
      WebshopUpdateManagement.UpdateSalesperson(Rec);
      GET(Code);
      COMMIT;

      CLEAR(WebshopWebform);
      //DynamicCommerceAddIn
      WebshopWebform.SetWebformType(WebformType::SalesPerson);
      WebshopWebform.SetRecordCode(Code);
      WebshopWebform.LOOKUPMODE(TRUE);
      WebshopWebform.RUNMODAL();

      GET(Code);
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce ---

      STANDARD-VERSION 2017

        ELEMENTS (0):
        PAGE ACTIONS (3):

          NAME                            CAPTION                         TYPE                 SUBTYPE

          E-Commerce                      <E-Commerce>                    ActionGroup
          Webshop Send Login Data         Zugangsdaten versenden          Action
          Webshop Update Salesperson      Verk�ufer Aktualisieren         Action


        TRIGGERS (0):
        GLOBAL VARIABLES (2):

          NAME                          DATATYPE        SUBTYPE                       LENGTH

          WebshopManagement             Codeunit        "Webshop Management"
          WebshopUpdateManagement       Codeunit        "Webshop Update Management"


        TEXT CONSTANTS (0):
        FUNCTIONS (0):


      INDIVIDUELLE ANPASSUNGEN [Kundenname]

        [YYYY-MM-TT] - [Mitarbeiterk�rzel]:
        Anpassung, ELEMENTS (0):, PAGE ACTIONS (0):, TRIGGERS (0):, GLOBAL VARIABLES (0):, TEXT CONSTANTS (0):, FUNCTIONS (0)


      +++ dynamic commerce +++
    }
    END.
  }

  CODE
  {
    VAR
      "--- MysydeShop ---"@1000000002 : Integer;
      WebshopManagement@1000000001 : Codeunit 5447300;
      WebshopUpdateManagement@1000000000 : Codeunit 5447301;

    PROCEDURE GetSelectionFilter@2() : Code[80];
    VAR
      Cust@1000 : Record 18;
      FirstCust@1001 : Code[30];
      LastCust@1002 : Code[30];
      SelectionFilter@1003 : Code[250];
      CustCount@1004 : Integer;
      More@1005 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      CurrPage.SETSELECTIONFILTER(Cust);
      CustCount := Cust.COUNT;
      IF CustCount > 0 THEN BEGIN
        Cust.FIND('-');
        WHILE CustCount > 0 DO BEGIN
          CustCount := CustCount - 1;
          Cust.MARKEDONLY(FALSE);
          FirstCust := Cust."No.";
          LastCust := FirstCust;
          More := (CustCount > 0);
          WHILE More DO
            IF Cust.NEXT = 0 THEN
              More := FALSE
            ELSE
              IF NOT Cust.MARK THEN
                More := FALSE
              ELSE BEGIN
                LastCust := Cust."No.";
                CustCount := CustCount - 1;
                IF CustCount = 0 THEN
                  More := FALSE;
              END;
          IF SelectionFilter <> '' THEN
            SelectionFilter := SelectionFilter + '|';
          IF FirstCust = LastCust THEN
            SelectionFilter := SelectionFilter + FirstCust
          ELSE
            SelectionFilter := SelectionFilter + FirstCust + '..' + LastCust;
          IF CustCount > 0 THEN BEGIN
            Cust.MARKEDONLY(TRUE);
            Cust.NEXT;
          END;
        END;
      END;
      EXIT(SelectionFilter);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetSelection@1(VAR Cust@1000 : Record 18);
    BEGIN
      CurrPage.SETSELECTIONFILTER(Cust);
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce ---

      STANDARD-VERSION 2017

        ELEMENTS (0):
        PAGE ACTIONS (6):

          NAME                            CAPTION                         TYPE                 SUBTYPE

          E-Commerce                      <E-Commerce>                    ActionGroup
          Webshop Send Login Data         Zugangsdaten versenden          Action
          Webshop Update Customer         Online Aktualisieren            Action
          Webshop Open Customer Login     Kundenzugang �ffnen             Action
          Page Webshop Permissions Group  Gruppenberechtigungen           Action
          Page Webshop Permissions Group  Artikelberechtigungen           Action


        TRIGGERS (0):
        GLOBAL VARIABLES (2):

          NAME                          DATATYPE        SUBTYPE                       LENGTH

          WebshopManagement             Codeunit        "Webshop Management"
          WebshopUpdateManagement       Codeunit        "Webshop Update Management"


        TEXT CONSTANTS (0):
        FUNCTIONS (0):


      INDIVIDUELLE ANPASSUNGEN [Kundenname]

        [YYYY-MM-TT] - [Mitarbeiterk�rzel]:
        Anpassung, ELEMENTS (0):, PAGE ACTIONS (0):, TRIGGERS (0):, GLOBAL VARIABLES (0):, TEXT CONSTANTS (0):, FUNCTIONS (0)


      +++ dynamic commerce +++
    }
    END.
  }
}

    PROCEDURE EnablePlanningControls@1();
    VAR
      PlanningGetParam@1000 : Codeunit 99000855;
      ReorderCycleEnabled@1010 : Boolean;
      SafetyLeadTimeEnabled@1009 : Boolean;
      SafetyStockQtyEnabled@1008 : Boolean;
      ReorderPointEnabled@1007 : Boolean;
      ReorderQuantityEnabled@1006 : Boolean;
      MaximumInventoryEnabled@1005 : Boolean;
      MinimumOrderQtyEnabled@1004 : Boolean;
      MaximumOrderQtyEnabled@1003 : Boolean;
      OrderMultipleEnabled@1002 : Boolean;
      IncludeInventoryEnabled@1001 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      PlanningGetParam.SetUpPlanningControls("Reordering Policy","Include Inventory",
        ReorderCycleEnabled,SafetyLeadTimeEnabled,SafetyStockQtyEnabled,
        ReorderPointEnabled,ReorderQuantityEnabled,MaximumInventoryEnabled,
        MinimumOrderQtyEnabled,MaximumOrderQtyEnabled,OrderMultipleEnabled,IncludeInventoryEnabled);
      "Reorder CycleEnable" := ReorderCycleEnabled;
      "Safety Lead TimeEnable" := SafetyLeadTimeEnabled;
      "Safety Stock QuantityEnable" := SafetyStockQtyEnabled;
      "Reorder PointEnable" := ReorderPointEnabled;
      "Reorder QuantityEnable" := ReorderQuantityEnabled;
      "Maximum InventoryEnable" := MaximumInventoryEnabled;
      "Minimum Order QuantityEnable" := MinimumOrderQtyEnabled;
      "Maximum Order QuantityEnable" := MaximumOrderQtyEnabled;
      "Order MultipleEnable" := OrderMultipleEnabled;
      "Include InventoryEnable" := IncludeInventoryEnabled;
    END;

    PROCEDURE EnableCostingControls@3();
    BEGIN
      "Standard CostEnable" := "Costing Method" = "Costing Method"::Standard;
      "Unit CostEnable" := "Costing Method" <> "Costing Method"::Standard;
    END;

    PROCEDURE GetSelectionFilter@2() : Code[80];
    VAR
      Item@1000 : Record 27;
      FirstItem@1001 : Code[30];
      LastItem@1002 : Code[30];
      SelectionFilter@1003 : Code[250];
      ItemCount@1004 : Integer;
      More@1005 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      CurrPage.SETSELECTIONFILTER(Item);
      ItemCount := Item.COUNT;
      IF ItemCount > 0 THEN BEGIN
        Item.FIND('-');
        WHILE ItemCount > 0 DO BEGIN
          ItemCount := ItemCount - 1;
          Item.MARKEDONLY(FALSE);
          FirstItem := Item."No.";
          LastItem := FirstItem;
          More := (ItemCount > 0);
          WHILE More DO
            IF Item.NEXT = 0 THEN
              More := FALSE
            ELSE
              IF NOT Item.MARK THEN
                More := FALSE
              ELSE BEGIN
                LastItem := Item."No.";
                ItemCount := ItemCount - 1;
                IF ItemCount = 0 THEN
                  More := FALSE;
              END;
          IF SelectionFilter <> '' THEN
            SelectionFilter := SelectionFilter + '|';
          IF FirstItem = LastItem THEN
            SelectionFilter := SelectionFilter + FirstItem
          ELSE
            SelectionFilter := SelectionFilter + FirstItem + '..' + LastItem;
          IF ItemCount > 0 THEN BEGIN
            Item.MARKEDONLY(TRUE);
            Item.NEXT;
          END;
        END;
      END;
      EXIT(SelectionFilter);
    END;

    PROCEDURE SetSelection@1(VAR Item@1000 : Record 27);
    BEGIN
      CurrPage.SETSELECTIONFILTER(Item);
    END;

    BEGIN
    {
      --- dynamic commerce ---

      STANDARD-VERSION 2017

        ELEMENTS (1):

          TYPE            SUBTYPE         SOURCEEXPR                      NAME                            CAPTION

          Field                           "Is Webshop Item"               <Is Webshop Item>               <Webshopartikel>


        PAGE ACTIONS (2):

          NAME                            CAPTION                         TYPE                 SUBTYPE

          <Action26>                      <E-Commerce>                    ActionGroup
          Webshop Item                    Webshop Artikel                 Action


        TRIGGERS (0):
        GLOBAL VARIABLES (0):
        TEXT CONSTANTS (0):
        FUNCTIONS (0):


      INDIVIDUELLE ANPASSUNGEN [Kundenname]

        [YYYY-MM-TT] - [Mitarbeiterk�rzel]:
        Anpassung, ELEMENTS (0):, PAGE ACTIONS (0):, TRIGGERS (0):, GLOBAL VARIABLES (0):, TEXT CONSTANTS (0):, FUNCTIONS (0)


      +++ dynamic commerce +++
    }
    END.
  }

      BilltoCustomerTemplateCodeEnab := "Bill-to Customer No." = '';
      SelltoCustomerTemplateCodeEnab := "Sell-to Customer No." = '';

      "Bill-to Customer No.Enable" := "Bill-to Customer Template Code" = '';
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE UpdateInfoPanel@4();
    VAR
      DifferSellToBillTo@1000 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      DifferSellToBillTo := "Sell-to Customer No." <> "Bill-to Customer No.";
      SalesHistoryBtnVisible := DifferSellToBillTo;
      BillToCommentPictVisible := DifferSellToBillTo;
      BillToCommentBtnVisible := DifferSellToBillTo;
      SalesHistoryStnVisible := SalesInfoPaneMgt.DocExist(Rec,"Sell-to Customer No.");
      IF DifferSellToBillTo THEN
        SalesHistoryBtnVisible := SalesInfoPaneMgt.DocExist(Rec,"Bill-to Customer No.")
    END;

    LOCAL PROCEDURE UpdateInfoPanel@2();
    VAR
      DifferSellToBillTo@1000 : Boolean;
    BEGIN
      DifferSellToBillTo := "Sell-to Customer No." <> "Bill-to Customer No.";
      SalesHistoryBtnVisible := DifferSellToBillTo;
      BillToCommentPictVisible := DifferSellToBillTo;
      BillToCommentBtnVisible := DifferSellToBillTo;
      SalesHistoryStnVisible := SalesInfoPaneMgt.DocExist(Rec,"Sell-to Customer No.");
      IF DifferSellToBillTo THEN
        SalesHistoryBtnVisible := SalesInfoPaneMgt.DocExist(Rec,"Bill-to Customer No.")
    END;

                      OnAction=VAR
                                 Text5447300@1000000000 : TextConst 'DEU=Kontakte an Newsletter-System senden;ENU=Send contacts to newsletter-system';
                                 WebshopSetup@1000000001 : Record 5447300;
                                 DefaultNumber@1000000002 : Integer;
                                 i@1000000003 : Integer;
                                 Shop@1000000004 : Record 5447301;
                                 OptionString@1000000005 : Text[1024];
                                 SelectedOption@1000000006 : Integer;
                                 Text5447301@1000000007 : TextConst 'DEU=Mit welchem Webshop sollen die Kontakte verkn�pft werden?;ENU=With what Shop should the contacts be synced?';
                               BEGIN
                                 //--- dynamic commerce ---
                                 IF Rec."Webshop Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
                                   WebshopSetup.GET;
                                   DefaultNumber := 1;

                                   IF Shop.COUNT > 1 THEN BEGIN
                                     i := 1;
                                     IF Shop.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                                       IF i <> 1 THEN
                                         OptionString += ',';
                                       OptionString += Shop.Description;
                                       IF Shop.Code = WebshopSetup."Default Shop Code" THEN
                                         DefaultNumber := i;
                                       i += 1;
                                     UNTIL Shop.NEXT = 0;
                                     SelectedOption := DIALOG.STRMENU(OptionString,DefaultNumber,Text5447301);
                                   END ELSE BEGIN
                                     SelectedOption := 1;
                                   END;
                                   IF SelectedOption > 0 THEN BEGIN
                                     IF SelectedOption = 1 THEN
                                       Shop.FINDFIRST
                                     ELSE BEGIN
                                       Shop.RESET;
                                       i := 1;
                                       IF Shop.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                                         i += 1;
                                       UNTIL (SelectedOption=i) OR (Shop.NEXT = 0);
                                     END;
                                   END;
                                 END ELSE
                                   Shop.GET("Webshop Code");

                                 IF (Rec."Webshop Code" <> '') OR (SelectedOption > 0) THEN BEGIN
                                   WebshopUpdateMgmt.StateIndicatorOpen(Text5447300);
                                   WebshopUpdateMgmt.NewsletterSegmentLineSyncAll(Rec,TRUE,Shop);
                                   WebshopUpdateMgmt.NewsletterSegmentLineSyncAll(Rec,FALSE,Shop);
                                   WebshopUpdateMgmt.StateIndicatorClose;
                                 END;
                               END;
                                }

                      CaptionML=[DEU=Kontakte mit E-Commerce Marketing synchronisieren;
                                 ENU=Synchronize Contacts with E-Commerce Marketing];
                      Promoted=Yes;
                      Image=TransmitElectronicDoc;
                      PromotedCategory=Process;
                      OnAction=VAR
                                 Text5447300@1000000000 : TextConst 'DEU=Kontakte an Newsletter-System senden;ENU=Send contacts to newsletter-system';
                                 WebshopSetup@1000000001 : Record 5447300;
                                 DefaultNumber@1000000002 : Integer;
                                 i@1000000003 : Integer;
                                 Shop@1000000004 : Record 5447301;
                                 OptionString@1000000005 : Text[1024];
                                 SelectedOption@1000000006 : Integer;
                                 Text5447301@1000000007 : TextConst 'DEU=Mit welchem Webshop sollen die Kontakte verkn�pft werden?;ENU=With what Shop should the contacts be synced?';
                               BEGIN
                                 //--- dynamic commerce ---
                                 IF Rec."Webshop Code" = '' THEN BEGIN
                                   WebshopSetup.GET;
                                   DefaultNumber := 1;

                                   IF Shop.COUNT > 1 THEN BEGIN
                                     i := 1;
                                     IF Shop.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                                       IF i <> 1 THEN
                                         OptionString += ',';
                                       OptionString += Shop.Description;
                                       IF Shop.Code = WebshopSetup."Default Shop Code" THEN
                                         DefaultNumber := i;
                                       i += 1;
                                     UNTIL Shop.NEXT = 0;
                                     SelectedOption := DIALOG.STRMENU(OptionString,DefaultNumber,Text5447301);
                                   END ELSE BEGIN
                                     SelectedOption := 1;
                                   END;
                                   IF SelectedOption > 0 THEN BEGIN
                                     IF SelectedOption = 1 THEN
                                       Shop.FINDFIRST
                                     ELSE BEGIN
                                       Shop.RESET;
                                       i := 1;
                                       IF Shop.FINDSET THEN REPEAT
                                         i += 1;
                                       UNTIL (SelectedOption=i) OR (Shop.NEXT = 0);
                                     END;
                                   END;
                                 END ELSE
                                   Shop.GET("Webshop Code");

                                 IF (Rec."Webshop Code" <> '') OR (SelectedOption > 0) THEN BEGIN
                                   WebshopUpdateMgmt.StateIndicatorOpen(Text5447300);
                                   WebshopUpdateMgmt.NewsletterSegmentLineSyncAll(Rec,TRUE,Shop);
                                   WebshopUpdateMgmt.NewsletterSegmentLineSyncAll(Rec,FALSE,Shop);
                                   WebshopUpdateMgmt.StateIndicatorClose;
                                 END;
                               END;
                                }
    }
